<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips)
  (:predicates
    (hand ?o)
    (cats ?o)
    (texture ?o)
    (vase ?o1 ?o2)
    (next ?o1 ?o2)
    (sneeze ?o)
    (stupendous ?o)
    (collect ?o1 ?o2)
    (spring ?o)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 ?o3)
    :precondition (and (sneeze ?o0) (texture ?o1) (texture ?o2) (stupendous ?o3) (next ?o0 ?o1) (collect ?o1 ?o3) (collect ?o2 ?o3))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (cats ?o0) (spring ?o1) (spring ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )
)
</domain>

DOMAIN

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips)
  (:predicates
    (hand ?o)
    (cats ?o)
    (texture ?o)
    (vase ?o1 ?o2)
    (next ?o1 ?o2)
    (sneeze ?o)
    (stupendous ?o)
    (collect ?o1 ?o2)
    (spring ?o)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 ?o3)
    :precondition (and (sneeze ?o0) (texture ?o1) (texture ?o2) (stupendous ?o3) (next ?o0 ?o1) (collect ?o1 ?o3) (collect ?o2 ?o3))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (cats ?o0) (spring ?o1) (spring ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of ensuring that both hand **object_7** and hand **object_8** are associated with texture **object_6**, follow these steps:

**Step 1: Re-associate Cat object_0 (00:00 - 00:01)**  
Perform a **memory** action to transition cat **object_0** from its current association with spring **object_6** to spring **object_5**. This aligns the cat with the current location of hand **object_8**.

**Step 2: Link Hand and Cat at Texture object_5 (00:01 - 00:02)**  
Execute a **sip** action using hand **object_8**, cat **object_0**, and texture **object_5**. This establishes a "vase" connection between the hand and the cat, effectively preparing hand **object_8** to be moved. Note that the hand is no longer considered "next" to texture **object_5** after this step.

**Step 3: Move Cat object_0 to the Destination (00:02 - 00:03)**  
Perform another **memory** action to move cat **object_0** back from spring **object_5** to spring **object_6**. This moves the cat (and the potential destination for the hand) to the target texture.

**Step 4: Relocate Hand object_8 (00:03 - 00:04)**  
Perform a **paltry** action using hand **object_8**, cat **object_0**, and texture **object_6**. This action utilizes the existing "vase" connection and the cat's position to place hand **object_8** "next" to texture **object_6**.

Upon completion, both **object_7** (which was already at texture **object_6**) and **object_8** are now associated with **object_6**, satisfying the goal conditions.

**Step 2: Perform the Sip action**
*   **Time:** T+1 minute
*   **Action:** Sip `object_7`, `object_0`, and `object_6`.
*   **Details:** Similar to the previous step, this action uses `object_7` (a hand), `object_0` (a cat), and `object_6` (a texture). It creates a `vase` connection between `object_7` and `object_0`, while removing the "next" link between `object_7` and `object_6`.

**Step 3: Perform the Memory action**
*   **Time:** T+2 minutes
*   **Action:** Memory `object_0`, `object_6`, and `object_5`.
*   **Details:** This action updates the state of `object_0` (the cat). It transitions the "next" relationship of `object_0` from `object_6` to `object_5`. This is possible because both `object_6` and `object_5` are categorized as "springs."

**Step 4: Perform the Paltry action**
*   **Time:** T+3 minutes
*   **Action:** Paltry `object_8`, `object_0`, and `object_5`.
*   **Details:** Using the `vase` connection established in Step 1 and the new "next" relationship for `object_0` established in Step 3, this action links `object_8` directly to `object_5` (the texture). The `vase` connection between `object_8` and `object_0` is dissolved.

**Step 5: Perform the Paltry action**
*   **Time:** T+4 minutes
*   **Action:** Paltry `object_7`, `object_0`, and `object_5`.
*   **Details:** Following the same logic as Step 4, this action links `object_7` directly to `object_5`. The `vase` connection between `object_7` and `object_0` is dissolved.

**Plan Execution Sequence:**

*   **Step 1: 00:00 - 00:01**
    Perform the **wretched** action using **object_3** (a sneeze entity), **object_5** (a texture), **object_6** (a texture), and **object_1** (a stupendous entity). This action shifts the connection of object_3 from object_5 to object_6.

*   **Step 2: 00:01 - 00:02**
    Perform the **clip** action using **object_9** (the hand), **object_3** (the sneeze entity), and **object_6** (the texture). This establishes a "vase" relationship between object_9 and object_3, effectively picking up or securing the object at that location.

*   **Step 3: 00:02 - 00:03**
    Perform another **wretched** action using **object_3**, **object_6**, **object_5**, and **object_1**. This shifts the connection of object_3 back from object_6 to object_5.

*   **Step 4: 00:03 - 00:04**
    Perform the **tightfisted** action using **object_9**, **object_3**, and **object_5**. This results in **object_9** now being positioned "next" to **object_5**, while releasing the "vase" bond with object_3.

*   **Step 5: 00:04 - 00:05**
    Perform the **sip** action using **object_9**, **object_0** (a cat entity), and **object_5**. This creates a "vase" relationship between the hand (**object_9**) and the cat (**object_0**) at the location of object_5.

*   **Step 6: 00:05 - 00:06**
    Perform the **memory** action using **object_0**, **object_5** (a spring entity), and **object_7** (another spring entity). This moves the target cat (**object_0**) from its position at object_5 to its new position at object_7.

*   **Step 7: 00:06 - 00:07**
    Perform the **paltry** action using **object_9**, **object_0**, and **object_7**. This final action moves the hand (**object_9**) to be "next" to **object_7** by utilizing the cat entity already at that location.

**Final Goal State:** The hand (**object_9**) is now successfully next to **object_7**.

Perform the **clip** action using the hand (**object_10**) to pick up the sneeze package (**object_5**) at location **object_9**. This action is possible because both the hand and the package are currently at that location.

**Step 2: Relocate sneeze package 5**
**Time:** 00:01 - 00:02
Execute the **wretched** action to move the sneeze package (**object_5**) from location **object_9** to location **object_8**. Both locations are part of the same region (region **object_3**).

**Step 3: Drop off sneeze package 5**
**Time:** 00:02 - 00:03
Perform the **tightfisted** action. The hand (**object_10**) drops the sneeze package (**object_5**) at its new location (**object_8**). This leaves the hand positioned at location **object_8**.

**Step 4: Pick up cat package 0**
**Time:** 00:03 - 00:04
Perform the **sip** action. Now that the hand (**object_10**) is at location **object_8**, it can pick up the cat package (**object_0**) which was already situated there.

**Step 5: Transfer cat package 0**
**Time:** 00:04 - 00:05
Execute the **memory** action to transfer the cat package (**object_0**) from spring location **object_8** to spring location **object_6**. 

**Step 6: Drop off cat package 0**
**Time:** 00:05 - 00:06
Finalize the plan with the **paltry** action. The hand (**object_10**) drops the cat package (**object_0**) at location **object_6**. 

**Final Result:** The hand (**object_10**) is now successfully positioned at **object_6**, satisfying the goal condition.
